class VerifyTransactionTest(FormAction):
    def get_url(self):
        return RUN_ON_URL

    def data_begin(self):
        # get username_reverse and password_reverse
        global username_approve, password_approve, username_reverse, password_reverse, username_login, password_login, username_approve_other_branch, password_approve_other_branch, username_reverse_other_branch, password_reverse_other_branch, username_other_branch, password_other_branch
        username_approve = USERNAME_APPROVE
        password_approve = PASSWORD_APPROVE
        username_reverse = USERNAME_REVERSE
        password_reverse = PASSWORD_REVERSE
        username_login = USERNAME_LOGIN
        password_login = PASSWORD_LOGIN
        username_approve_other_branch = USERNAME_APPROVE_OTHER_BRANCH
        password_approve_other_branch = PASSWORD_APPROVE_OTHER_BRANCH
        username_reverse_other_branch = USERNAME_REVERSE_OTHER_BRANCH
        password_reverse_other_branch = PASSWORD_REVERSE_OTHER_BRANCH
        username_other_branch = USERNAME_LOGIN_OTHER_BRANCH
        password_other_branch = PASSWORD_LOGIN_OTHER_BRANCH
        self.login(username_login, password_login, one_app=ONE_APP)
        global working_date, branch_code
        working_date = self.get_working_date()
        branch_code = self.get_logged_branch_code()
        global gl_account_number
        gl_account_number = f'{branch_code}-1100601000000-01'

    def start_class(self):
        self.data_begin()

    def end_class(self):
        self.logout()

    def reset_browser(self):
        self.close_popup()
        self.logout()
        self.restart_browser()
        self.data_begin()

# Check the data used for testing
    def test_000_01_check_test_data_must_exist(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        self.add_gl_level_9_use_for_testing(
            branch_code=branch_code,
            currency_code='MMK',
            account_number=gl_account_number
        )
        if self.check_customer_profile_not_exist(customer_code_personal):
            self.stop()
            self.fail()

# Create current deposit account use for testing
    def test_000_02_create_current_deposit_account_use_for_testing(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        global deposit_current_pending
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_current_pending=dpt_opn_result[1]
        global deposit_current_new
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_current_new=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_current_new,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_cq, stock_type_cq, 24)
        print(f'generated_number new: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_cq,
            number_of_leaves=number_of_leaves_cq,
            number_of_book=number_of_book_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        from_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[1]
        to_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )
        self.dpt_ccr(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cis(
            account_number=deposit_current_new,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        global deposit_current_normal
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_current_normal=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_current_normal,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cdp(
            account_number=deposit_current_normal,
            amount_deposit=deposit_amount_current,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_cq, stock_type_cq, 24)
        print(f'generated_number normal: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_cq,
            number_of_leaves=number_of_leaves_cq,
            number_of_book=number_of_book_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        from_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[1]
        to_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )
        self.dpt_ccr(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cis(
            account_number=deposit_current_normal,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        global deposit_current_dormant
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_current_dormant=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cdp(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            amount_deposit=deposit_amount_current,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cas(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            new_status=status_dormant,
            current_status=status_normal,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_cq, stock_type_cq, 24)
        print(f'generated_number dormant: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_cq,
            number_of_leaves=number_of_leaves_cq,
            number_of_book=number_of_book_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        from_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[1]
        to_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )
        self.dpt_ccr(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cis(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        global deposit_current_block
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,

            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_current_block=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_current_block,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cdp(
            account_number=deposit_current_block,
            amount_deposit=deposit_amount_current,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_cq, stock_type_cq, 24)
        print(f'generated_number block: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_cq,
            number_of_leaves=number_of_leaves_cq,
            number_of_book=number_of_book_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        from_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[1]
        to_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )
        self.dpt_ccr(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',

            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cis(
            account_number=deposit_current_block,

        self.dpt_blk(

# DPT_CSH - INVALID CASE
    def test_001_dpt_csh_01_check_not_allowed_deposit_type_fixed_deposit(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'InvalidDpttype: Invalid deposit type of account [{self.no_mask(deposit_fixed_01m_normal)}]- en'
        ]
        self.dpt_csh(
            account_number_debit=deposit_fixed_01m_normal,
            enter_side='D',
            debit_amount=amount_fixed_01m_valid,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )

    def test_001_dpt_csh_02_check_not_allowed_deposit_type_prepaid(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'InvalidDpttype: Invalid deposit type of account [{self.no_mask(deposit_prepaid_normal)}]- en'
        ]
        self.dpt_csh(
            account_number_debit=deposit_prepaid_normal,
            enter_side='D',
            debit_amount=amount_prepaid_valid,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )

    def test_001_dpt_csh_03_check_not_allowed_deposit_status_block(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'InvalidDepositStatus: Invalid deposit status [{status_block}]'
        ]
        self.dpt_csh(
            account_number_debit=deposit_current_block,
            enter_side='D',
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )
        self.dpt_csh(
            account_number_debit=deposit_savings_block,
            enter_side='D',
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )

    def test_001_dpt_csh_04_check_not_allowed_deposit_status_closed(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'InvalidDepositStatus: Invalid deposit status [{status_closed}]'

# DPT_CWR - VALID CASE
    def test_004_dpt_cwr_10_check_allowed_deposit_type_savings(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )
        dpt_cwr_result = self.dpt_cwr(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            withdraw_amount=amount_savings_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwr_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwr_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )

    def test_004_dpt_cwr_11_handle_dormant_savings(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_cwr_result = self.dpt_cwr(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            withdraw_amount=amount_savings_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwr_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )
        self.dpt_cas(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            new_status=status_dormant,
            current_status=status_normal,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )

    def test_004_dpt_cwr_12_restart_browser(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        self.reset_browser()

        dpt_mwr_result = self.dpt_mwr(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            withdraw_amount=amount_savings_valid,
            credit_accounting=gl_account_number,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_mwr_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_mwr_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )

    def test_005_dpt_mwr_17_handle_dormant_current(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_mwr_result = self.dpt_mwr(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            withdraw_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_accounting=gl_account_number,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_mwr_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        # verify deposit account

            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )
        self.dpt_cas(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            new_status=status_dormant,
            current_status=status_normal,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )

# DPT_TRF - VALID CASE
    def test_006_dpt_trf_34_check_allowed_deposit_type_current(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        dpt_trf_result = self.dpt_trf(
            debit_account=deposit_current_normal,
            amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=other_current_deposit_account,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_trf_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_trf_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        dpt_trf_result = self.dpt_trf(
            debit_account=other_current_deposit_account,
            amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_current_normal,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_trf_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_trf_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )

    def test_006_dpt_trf_36_handle_dormant_current(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_trf_result = self.dpt_trf(
            debit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=other_current_deposit_account,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_trf_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )
        self.dpt_cas(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            new_status=status_dormant,
            current_status=status_normal,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        list_error_message = [
            'DormantNotAllowedCredit: Not Allowed to do Credit Posting because this Account is Dormant'
        ]
        self.dpt_trf(
            debit_account=other_current_deposit_account,
            amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

    def test_006_dpt_trf_37_handle_dormant_savings(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_trf_result = self.dpt_trf(
            debit_account=deposit_savings_dormant,
            amount=amount_savings_valid,
            credit_account=other_savings_deposit_account,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_trf_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )
        self.dpt_cas(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            new_status=status_dormant,
            current_status=status_normal,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        list_error_message = [
            'DormantNotAllowedCredit: Not Allowed to do Credit Posting because this Account is Dormant'
        ]
        self.dpt_trf(
            debit_account=other_savings_deposit_account,
            amount=amount_savings_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_savings_dormant,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

    def test_006_dpt_trf_38_restart_browser(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        self.reset_browser()

        print(f'generated_number: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_cq,
            number_of_leaves=number_of_leaves_cq,
            number_of_book=number_of_book_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        from_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[1]
        to_serial_cq = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )
        self.dpt_ccr(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )

# DPT_CER - INVALID CASE
    def test_009_dpt_cer_00_create_data_use_for_testing(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        global deposit_prepaid_normal_valid
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_prepaid,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening',
            to_account_number=other_current_deposit_account,
        )
        deposit_prepaid_normal_valid=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_prepaid_normal_valid,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cdp(
            account_number=deposit_prepaid_normal_valid,
            amount_deposit=deposit_amount_prepaid,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        global from_serial_fr, to_serial_fr
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_fr, stock_type_fr, 0)
        print(f'generated_number: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_fr,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_fr,
            number_of_leaves=number_of_leaves_fr,
            number_of_book=number_of_book_fr,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        from_serial_fr = dpt_srg_result[1]
        to_serial_fr = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(
            stock_type=stock_type_fr,
            from_serial=from_serial_fr,
            to_serial=to_serial_fr,
            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )
        self.dpt_ccr(
            stock_type=stock_type_fr,
            from_serial=from_serial_fr,
            to_serial=to_serial_fr,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        global deposit_prepaid_new_no_link
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_prepaid,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening',
            to_account_number=other_current_deposit_account,
        )
        deposit_prepaid_new_no_link=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_prepaid_new_no_link,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_account_linkage_delete(
            master_account_number=deposit_prepaid_new_no_link
        )
        # verify deposit account after
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_prepaid_new_no_link,
            linkage_account_number='',
        )
        global deposit_prepaid_pending_no_link
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_prepaid,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening',
            to_account_number=other_current_deposit_account,
        )
        deposit_prepaid_pending_no_link=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_account_linkage_delete(
            master_account_number=deposit_prepaid_pending_no_link
        )
        # verify deposit account after
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_prepaid_pending_no_link,
            linkage_account_number='',
        )

    def test_011_dpt_sbi_00_create_data_use_for_testing(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        global deposit_savings_normal_valid
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_savings,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_savings_normal_valid=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal_valid,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cdp(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal_valid,
            amount_deposit=deposit_amount_savings,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        global from_serial_sb, to_serial_sb
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_sb, stock_type_sb, 0)
        print(f'generated_number: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_sb,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_sb,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        from_serial_sb = dpt_srg_result[1]
        to_serial_sb = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(
            stock_type=stock_type_sb,
            from_serial=from_serial_sb,
            to_serial=to_serial_sb,
            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )
        self.dpt_ccr(
            stock_type=stock_type_sb,
            from_serial=from_serial_sb,
            to_serial=to_serial_sb,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )

    def test_012_dpt_cei_00_create_data_use_for_testing(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        global deposit_current_new_dpt_cei, from_serial_cq_new, to_serial_cq_new
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_current_new_dpt_cei=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_current_new_dpt_cei,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_cq, stock_type_cq, 24)
        print(f'generated_number new: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_cq,
            number_of_leaves=number_of_leaves_cq,
            number_of_book=number_of_book_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )

        to_serial_cq_new = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(

            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )

        to_serial = generated_number[1]

        to_serial = generated_number[1]

            stock_type=stock_type_cq,

        to_serial_cq_dormant = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(

            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )
        self.dpt_ccr(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,

    def test_012_dpt_cei_05_check_available_balance_invalid_normal(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'Invalid available balance of account [{self.no_mask(deposit_current_normal_dpt_cei)}]. Available balance must be more than [{self.format_number(amount_current_invalid)}] - en'
        ]
        dpt_cei_result = self.dpt_cei(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal,
            cheque_amount=amount_current_invalid,
            account_number=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cei,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cei_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=dpt_cei_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )

    def test_012_dpt_cei_06_fee_check_available_balance_invalid_normal(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'InvalidAvailableAmt: Invalid available balance of account [{self.no_mask(deposit_current_normal_dpt_cei)}]. Available balance must be more than [{self.format_number(value_invalid)}] - en'
        ]
        self.dpt_cei(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal,
            cheque_amount=amount_current_valid,
            account_number=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cei,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_invalid,
        )

# DPT_CEI - VALID CASE
    def test_012_dpt_cei_07_fee_check_allowed_normal(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        dpt_cei_result = self.dpt_cei(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal,
            cheque_amount=amount_current_valid,
            account_number=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cei,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=value_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cei_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cei_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )

        self.reset_browser()

        global deposit_current_new_dpt_cwc, from_serial_cq_new_dpt_cwc, to_serial_cq_new_dpt_cwc

            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_current_new_dpt_cwc=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_current_new_dpt_cwc,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_cq, stock_type_cq, 24)
        print(f'generated_number new: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_cq,
            number_of_leaves=number_of_leaves_cq,
            number_of_book=number_of_book_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )

        to_serial_cq_new_dpt_cwc = dpt_srg_result[2]

            stock_type=stock_type_cq,

            to_serial=to_serial_cq_new_dpt_cwc,

            values=values_invalid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwc_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwc_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )

# DPT_CWC - VALID CASE - 1121: Cash withdrawal by cheque
    def test_014_dpt_cwc_06_fee_check_allowed_normal(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        dpt_cwc_result = self.dpt_cwc(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cwc,
            cheque_amount=amount_current_valid,
            account_number=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cwc,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwc_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwc_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )

    def test_014_dpt_cwc_07_handle_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cwc,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_cwc_result = self.dpt_cwc(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cwc,
            cheque_amount=amount_current_valid,
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cwc,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwc_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cwc,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwc_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cwc,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

    def test_014_dpt_cwc_08_restart_browser(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        self.reset_browser()

        )

            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cis(
            account_number=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cdt,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        global deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt, from_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt, to_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cdp(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            amount_deposit=deposit_amount_current,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cas(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            new_status=status_dormant,
            current_status=status_normal,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_cq, stock_type_cq, 24)
        print(f'generated_number dormant: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_cq,
            number_of_leaves=number_of_leaves_cq,
            number_of_book=number_of_book_cq,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        from_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt = dpt_srg_result[1]
        to_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt = dpt_srg_result[2]
        self.dpt_sat(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            assigned_staff_code=username_login
        )
        self.dpt_ccr(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.dpt_cis(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            from_serial=from_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            to_serial=to_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        global deposit_current_block_dpt_cdt, from_serial_cq_block_dpt_cdt, to_serial_cq_block_dpt_cdt
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,

            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,

        )
        deposit_current_block_dpt_cdt=dpt_opn_result[1]

        )
        self.dpt_cdp(
            account_number=deposit_current_block_dpt_cdt,

            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        generated_number = self.gen_serial_number(stock_prefix_cq, stock_type_cq, 24)
        print(f'generated_number block: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(
            stock_type=stock_type_cq,
            from_serial=from_serial,
            to_serial=to_serial,
            stock_prefix=stock_prefix_cq,

        )

        global deposit_current_close_dpt_cdt, from_serial_cq_close_dpt_cdt, to_serial_cq_close_dpt_cdt

        print(f'generated_number close: {generated_number}')
        from_serial = generated_number[0]
        to_serial = generated_number[1]
        dpt_srg_result = self.dpt_srg(

    def test_015_dpt_cdt_06_debit_fee_check_fee_amount_greater_than_debit_amount(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'Invalid amount [-9.99]'
        ]
        dpt_cdt_result = self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_current_normal,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=['10.00'],
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        dpt_cdt_result = self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_savings_normal,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=['10.00'],
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )

    def test_015_dpt_cdt_11_credit_check_not_allowed_deposit_status_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            'DormantNotAllowedCredit: Not Allowed to do Credit Posting because this Account is Dormant'
        ]
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_cdt_result = self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_savings_dormant,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

# DPT_CDT (Debit account) - VALID CASE - 1111: Deposit by cheque
    def test_015_dpt_cdt_12_debit_fee_check_allowed_normal(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        dpt_cdt_result = self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_current_normal,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        dpt_cdt_result = self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_savings_normal,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )

    def test_015_dpt_cdt_13_debit_handle_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_cdt_result = self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_current_normal,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        dpt_cdt_result = self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_savings_normal,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account

            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cdt,

# DPT_CDT (Credit account) - VALID CASE - 1111: Deposit by cheque
    def test_015_dpt_cdt_14_credit_check_allowed_deposit_status_new_current(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        dpt_opn_result = self.dpt_opn(
            customer_code=customer_code_personal,
            customer_type='Single customer',
            catalogue_code=catalogue_code_current,
            reason_of_account_opening='Enter value reason of account opening'
        )
        deposit_current_new_credit=dpt_opn_result[1]
        self.dpt_apr(
            account_number=deposit_current_new_credit,
            approve_on_form='Y',
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        list_error_message = [
            f'InvalidAvailableAmt: Invalid available balance of account [{self.no_mask(deposit_current_new_credit)}]. Available balance must be more than [0.00] - en'
        ]
        self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_current_new_credit,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            list_error_message=list_error_message,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_new_credit,
            account_status=status_new,
            current_balance='0.00',
        )
        self.bo_click_tab('Balance information')
        amount_valid = self.bo_get_value('Initial deposit amount')
        dpt_cdt_result = self.dpt_cdt(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_account=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cdt,
            debit_amount=amount_valid,
            credit_account=deposit_current_new_credit,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_new_credit,
            account_status=status_normal,
            current_balance=amount_valid,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cdt_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_new_credit,
            account_status=status_new,
            current_balance='0.00',
        )

# DPT_CWM - VALID CASE - 1125: Misellaneous debit by cheque
    def test_016_dpt_cwm_06_fee_check_allowed_normal(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        dpt_cwm_result = self.dpt_cwm(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_normal_dpt_cwm,
            account_number=deposit_current_normal_dpt_cwm,
            cheque_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_accounting=gl_account_number,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwm_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwm_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )

    def test_016_dpt_cwm_07_handle_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cwm,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_cwm_result = self.dpt_cwm(
            cheque_no=from_serial_cq_dormant_dpt_cwm,
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cwm,
            cheque_amount=amount_current_valid,
            credit_accounting=gl_account_number,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwm_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cwm,
            account_status=status_normal,
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cwm_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant_dpt_cwm,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

    def test_016_dpt_cwm_08_restart_browser(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        self.reset_browser()

        list_error_message = [
            f'Invalid status [{status_dormant}] of account [{self.no_mask(deposit_current_dormant)}]'
        ]
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_cci_result = self.dpt_cci(
            serial_no=from_serial_gc,
            amount=amount_savings_valid,
            debit_method='Deposit',
            debit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cci_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=dpt_cci_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_cci_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_cci_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )

        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        self.reset_browser()

    def test_018_dpt_ccw_25_check_not_allowed_deposit_type_current_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'Invalid account status [{status_dormant}] of [{self.no_mask(deposit_current_dormant)}]'
        ]
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_ccw_result = self.dpt_ccw(
            serial_no=from_serial_gc,
            withdrawal_method='Deposit',
            credit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_ccw_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=dpt_ccw_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

    def test_019_dpt_rcc_25_check_not_allowed_deposit_type_current_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        global trans_ref_dpt_rcc_current_dormant
        list_error_message = [
            f'Invalid account status [{status_dormant}] of [{self.no_mask(deposit_current_dormant)}]'
        ]
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_rcc_result = self.dpt_rcc(
            serial_no=from_serial_gc,
            return_method='Deposit',
            credit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        trans_ref_dpt_rcc_current_dormant = dpt_rcc_result[0]
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=trans_ref_dpt_rcc_current_dormant, 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=trans_ref_dpt_rcc_current_dormant, 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

    def test_019_dpt_rcc_26_check_not_allowed_deposit_type_savings_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # check transaction status here, because trans_ref_dpt_rcc_current_dormant failed
        if self.check_transaction(trans_ref_dpt_rcc_current_dormant, 'Completed'):
            # verify deposit account
            self.deposit_account_view(
                account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
                account_status=status_normal,
            )
            self.transaction_reverse(
                transaction_references=trans_ref_dpt_rcc_current_dormant, 
                username=username_reverse,
                password=password_reverse
            )
            # verify deposit account
            self.deposit_account_view(
                account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
                account_status=status_dormant,
            )
        # process for this case
        global trans_ref_dpt_rcc_savings_dormant
        list_error_message = [
            f'Invalid account status [{status_dormant}] of [{self.no_mask(deposit_savings_dormant)}]'
        ]
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_rcc_result = self.dpt_rcc(
            serial_no=from_serial_gc,
            return_method='Deposit',
            credit_account=deposit_savings_dormant,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        trans_ref_dpt_rcc_savings_dormant = dpt_rcc_result[0]
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=trans_ref_dpt_rcc_savings_dormant, 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=trans_ref_dpt_rcc_savings_dormant, 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

# DPT_RCC - VALID CASE - 11808: Gift cheque return
    def test_019_dpt_rcc_29_fee_check_allowed_deposit_type_current_normal(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # check transaction status here, because trans_ref_dpt_rcc_savings_dormant_fee failed
        if self.check_transaction(trans_ref_dpt_rcc_savings_dormant_fee, 'Completed'):
        # verify deposit account
            self.deposit_account_view(
                account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
                account_status=status_normal,
            )
            self.transaction_reverse(
                transaction_references=trans_ref_dpt_rcc_savings_dormant_fee, 
                username=username_reverse,
                password=password_reverse
            )
            # verify deposit account
            self.deposit_account_view(
                account_number=deposit_savings_dormant,
                account_status=status_dormant,
            )
        # process for this case
        dpt_rcc_result = self.dpt_rcc(
            serial_no=from_serial_gc,
            return_method='Deposit',
            credit_account=deposit_current_normal,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_rcc_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_rcc_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )

    def test_020_dpt_poi_29_fee_check_not_allowed_deposit_type_current_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'Invalid status [{status_dormant}] of account [{self.no_mask(deposit_current_dormant)}]'
        ]
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_poi_result = self.dpt_poi(
            serial_no=from_serial_po,
            amount=amount_savings_valid,
            debit_method='Deposit',
            debit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_poi_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=dpt_poi_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

    def test_020_dpt_poi_30_fee_check_not_allowed_deposit_type_savings_dormant(self):

            debit_account=deposit_current_normal,
            ifc_codes=ifc_codes,
            values=values_valid,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_poi_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_poi_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )

    def test_021_dpt_pow_25_check_not_allowed_deposit_type_current_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'Invalid account status [{status_dormant}] of [{self.no_mask(deposit_current_dormant)}]'
        ]
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_pow_result = self.dpt_pow(
            serial_no=from_serial_po,
            withdrawal_method='Deposit',
            credit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_pow_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=dpt_pow_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse

    def test_022_dpt_rpo_25_check_not_allowed_deposit_type_current_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        list_error_message = [
            f'Invalid status [{status_dormant}] of account [{self.no_mask(deposit_current_dormant)}]'
        ]
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_rpo_result = self.dpt_rpo(
            serial_no=from_serial_po,
            return_method='Deposit',
            credit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            approve_later='Y',
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_rpo_result[0],
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=list_error_message
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=dpt_rpo_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

    def test_024_dpt_dls_38_other_02_check_not_allowed_same_account(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        dpt_dls_result = self.dpt_dls(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            another_deposit_account=deposit_savings_normal,
            approve_later='Y'
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_dls_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve,
            allow_approve='N',
            list_error_message=[f'Not allow to use same account']
            
        )
        self.transaction_reject(
            transaction_references=dpt_dls_result[0], 
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )

    def test_024_dpt_dls_39_other_check_allowed_deposit_status_current_dormant(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )
        dpt_dls_result = self.dpt_dls(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            another_deposit_account=deposit_current_dormant,
            approve_later='Y'
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_dls_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_dls_result[0],
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_dormant,
            account_status=status_dormant,
        )

    def test_024_dpt_dls_40_other_check_allowed_deposit_status_current_new(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_new,
            account_status=status_new,
        )
        dpt_dls_result = self.dpt_dls(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            another_deposit_account=deposit_current_new,
            approve_later='Y'
        )
        self.transaction_approve(
            transaction_references=dpt_dls_result[0], 
            username=username_approve,
            password=password_approve
        )
        self.transaction_reverse(
            transaction_references=dpt_dls_result[0],
            username=username_reverse,
            password=password_reverse
        )
        # verify deposit account
        self.deposit_account_view(
            account_number=deposit_current_new,
            account_status=status_new,
        )

        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        self.dpt_mls_error(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            error_message=f'Only the branch holding the deposit account can be made'
        )
        self.dpt_mls_error(
            account_number=deposit_savings_normal,
            accounting_number=gl_account_number,
            list_error_message=[f'BranchNotAllow: Only the branch holding the deposit account can be made']
        )

    def test_029_dpt_mls_03_check_not_allowed_other_branch_fixed_deposit(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        self.dpt_mls_error(
            account_number=deposit_fixed_01m_normal,
            error_message=f'Only the branch holding the deposit account can be made'
        )
        self.dpt_mls_error(
            account_number=deposit_fixed_01m_normal,
            accounting_number=gl_account_number,
            list_error_message=[f'BranchNotAllow: Only the branch holding the deposit account can be made']
        )

    def test_029_dpt_mls_04_check_not_allowed_other_branch_prepaid(self):
        print('Start: ' + datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S'))
        self.dpt_mls_error(
            account_number=deposit_prepaid_normal,
            error_message=f'Only the branch holding the deposit account can be made'
        )
        self.dpt_mls_error(
            account_number=deposit_prepaid_normal,
            accounting_number=gl_account_number,
            list_error_message=[f'BranchNotAllow: Only the branch holding the deposit account can be made']
        )
